    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""

big = False, kills = False

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))

big = False, kills = False

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))

work = <test_outputs.Work object at 0xfe4c49bbc690>, tag = 'one', legacy = None
big = False, kills = False

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))
E           Failed: /app/ledger/rebuild exited with [REDACTED]27
E           stdout tail:

E           env: ‘/app/ledger/rebuild’: No such file or directory

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""

            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)

    def run_agent(events_path, state_in, as_of, work, tag, legacy=None, big=False, kills=False):
        """Run the agent program as the runner user inside its own directory.
    
        The peak resident memory of every process owned by runner is sampled
        while the program runs and must stay under MEM_LIMIT_BYTES.
    
        kills: interruption mode (policy section 7.4). The program is sent
        SIGKILL every KILL_INTERVAL seconds and started again with the same
        command line, until it exits on its own. A program that does not save
        durable progress never finishes and fails after KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS."""
        timeout = BIG_RUN_TIMEOUT if big else RUN_TIMEOUT
        d = work.agent_dir(tag)
        events = os.path.join(d, "events.jsonl")
        shutil.copyfile(events_path, events)
        _give(events)
        legacy_copy = None
        if legacy:
            legacy_copy = os.path.join(d, "legacy-ledger.jsonl")
            shutil.copyfile(legacy, legacy_copy)
            _give(legacy_copy)
        state_out = os.path.join(d, "state.json")
        ledger = os.path.join(d, "ledger.jsonl")
        report = os.path.join(d, "report.json")
        cmd = ["runuser", "-u", RUNNER, "--", "env", "-i",
               "HOME=" + d, "PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin",
               "LANG=C.UTF-8", "TMPDIR=" + d,
               AGENT, "--events", events, "--state-out", state_out,
               "--ledger-out", ledger, "--report-out", report]
        if state_in:
            state_copy = os.path.join(d, "state_in.json")
            shutil.copyfile(state_in, state_copy)
            _give(state_copy)
            cmd += ["--state-in", state_copy]
        for p in (state_out, ledger, report):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        if as_of:
            cmd += ["--as-of", as_of]
        if legacy_copy:
            cmd += ["--legacy-ledger", legacy_copy]
        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        else:
            with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                try:
                    rc = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds" % (AGENT, timeout))
                finally:
                    _kill_runner_processes(proc)
        peak = monitor.stop()
        if peak > MEM_LIMIT_BYTES:
            pytest.fail("%s used %d MB of resident memory at its peak, the limit is %d MB" % (
                AGENT, peak // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024), MEM_LIMIT_BYTES // ([REDACTED]024 * [REDACTED]024)))
        if rc != 0:
            with open(out_path) as fh:
                out_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
            with open(err_path) as fh:
                err_tail = fh.read()[-2000:]
>           pytest.fail("%s exited with %d\nstdout tail:\n%s\nstderr tail:\n%s" % (AGENT, rc, out_tail, err_tail))

        # Output goes to files, not pipes, so a child that keeps a pipe open cannot
        # stall the verifier. The program runs in its own session so the whole
        # group can be killed.
        out_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stdout")
        err_path = os.path.join(work.ref, tag + ".agent.stderr")
        monitor = MemoryMonitor().start()
        rc = None
        if kills:
            started = time.time()
            for attempt in range(KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
                with open(out_path, "a") as out_fh, open(err_path, "a") as err_fh:
                    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=d, stdout=out_fh, stderr=err_fh, start_new_session=True)
                    try:
                        rc = proc.wait(timeout=KILL_INTERVAL)
                        break
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                    finally:
                        _kill_runner_processes(proc)
                if time.time() - started > KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT:
                    monitor.stop()
                    pytest.fail("%s did not finish within %d seconds under repeated interruption" % (AGENT, KILL_TOTAL_TIMEOUT))
            if rc is None:
                monitor.stop()
                pytest.fail("%s never finished under repeated interruption: killed every %d seconds, %d restarts" % (
                    AGENT, KILL_INTERVAL, KILL_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
